# automath documentation inventory — 2026-09-09

Snapshot assembled on 2026-09-09; recently changed files refreshed at approximately 04:21 CDT (09:21 UTC). This is a live workspace, not an atomic archival snapshot. Later edits can change hashes and status.

## Scope and review meaning

2272 files, 57,959,358 bytes, were enumerated and mechanically scanned. This index is NOT a claim that every document has been semantically read. Current instructions, handoff material, active problem notes, and selected proof/checking entry points received focused review; older dispatches, repeated exports, collected papers, and binary documents were primarily indexed. See [the handoff review](CODEX_HANDOFF_20260909.md) for what was actually checked.

Roots:

- Project: `$HOME/workspace/claudecode/automath`
- Claude project memory: `$HOME/.claude/projects/-Users-user-workspace-claudecode-automath/memory`

Formats: Markdown, reStructuredText, AsciiDoc, TeX, PDF, DOCX, Org, and HTML. Hashes cover raw bytes, including binary PDFs; hashing a PDF is not reading it. Code, plaintext logs, JSON, solver certificates and numerical datasets are not counted as documentation here, although several relevant examples were separately inspected or executed.

Excluded: sibling projects in the parent `claudecode` workspace; `.git`, `.lake`, `.venv`, `.pixi`, `node_modules`, `site-packages`, `.gstack`, `engine/vendor`, the `tools` subtree (vendored/build tools), and upstream `problems/formal-conjectures` / `problems/erdosproblems` checkouts. The active custom watchdog script was inspected separately. The newly created Codex review and this generated index are not counted in their own input snapshot.
